## Corpus Check
- 2904 files · ~10,966,994 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 470 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .glb 47, .css 32, .mtlx 28)

## Summary
- 36911 nodes · 69965 edges · 1732 communities (1409 shown, 323 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3200 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5c618cb1`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
